# FFDC Analyzer: Source and Artifact Inventory

Baseline generated by `docs/_tools/audit_project.py`. Full bytes were read for every regular file in the stated scope; no error was reported. Binary contents are not treated as source and are not reproduced. `docs/` is an explicitly excluded generated output, and Git internals are not part of application semantics.

| Regular files read | 1904 |
| Bytes read | 280709884 |
| Root Python/current/historical modules fully parsed | 49 |
| Current runtime callables | 512 |
| Read/parse errors | 0 |
| Machine-readable manifest | _audit/structure.json |
| Test evidence | _audit/validation.json |

## File Responsibilities and Relationships

| Group | Responsibility / relationship |
| --- | --- |
| app.py | Product entrypoint; imports companion Python modules and emits classic HTML; references static assets. |
| ffdc_chat.py | Shared evidence tools and model orchestration; inherited by workspace_analysis. |
| workspace_analysis.py | Focused evidence and verification; calls base EvidenceTools. |
| workspace_ui.py | HTML shell; references workspace JavaScript/CSS, Marked, Lucide and logo. |
| ffdc_config.py | Bounded environment parsing and path/URL normalization. |
| ffdc_jobs.py | Atomic background-job snapshots, restart recovery and in-memory expiry cleanup. |
| static/ | First-party workspace assets plus separately licensed Marked/Lucide bundles. |
| test_*.py | Runtime mocked unit tests; legacy monitor tests import app.py.jira_monitor. |
| app.py.* | Historical copies; not active imports except the explicit legacy test loader. |
| compare_*, inspect_*, extract_*, summarize_*, map_*, deep_compare_*, focused_* | One-off case investigation scripts; often hardcoded external log paths; not called by app main. |
| patch_* | Historical source-rewrite utilities; importing/executing can modify external app files. |
| v4_batch_validate.py / vision_capability_test.py | Manual validation/probe scripts; may call filesystem/network services. |
| nginx-ffdc-analyzer.conf | FFDC proxy template for port 8088. |
| 00-installer-config.yaml | Host installation/network configuration, not read by the analyzer. |
| knowledgebase_patch/ and nginx-knowledgebase.conf | Separate Knowledge Base service artifacts; not FFDC runtime. |
| reports/, uploads/, ffdc_cache/, jira_monitor_jobs/ | Runtime/generated data; do not treat case contents as code or embed them in documentation. |
| work/, outputs/, ppt_work/, ppt_repair/, deliverables/ | Development, diagnostic, presentation/video artifacts; not required for server startup. |
| __pycache__/ | Generated Python bytecode, not source. |
